        kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
            try {
                block()
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                t.printStackTrace()
                Toast.makeText(applicationContext, "Saved to board", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                finish()
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun ensureDefaultBoardsExist(dao: com.example.data.local.BoardDao) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val dump = dao.getBoardById("board_dump")
            if (dump == null) {
                dao.insertBoard(
                    BoardEntity(
                        id = "board_dump",
                        title = "Dump board",
                        slug = "dump",
                        ownerUsername = "you",
                        ownerDisplayName = "Dump board",
                        ownerAvatarUrl = "",
                        pinCount = 0,
                        followerCount = 0,
                        boardUrl = "custom://dump",
                        description = "Default dump board for quick saves and fallback",
                        isCustom = true,
                        customDomains = "",
                        isDefault = true,
                        isSpecialNonDeletable = true
                    )
                )
                val mySaveExisting = dao.getBoardById("board_my_save")
                if (mySaveExisting != null && mySaveExisting.isDefault) {
                    dao.insertBoard(mySaveExisting.copy(isDefault = false))
                }
            }
            val mySave = dao.getBoardById("board_my_save")
            if (mySave == null) {
                dao.insertBoard(
                    BoardEntity(
                        id = "board_my_save",
                        title = "My Save",
                        slug = "my-save",
                        ownerUsername = "you",
                        ownerDisplayName = "My Save",
                        ownerAvatarUrl = "",
                        pinCount = 0,
                        followerCount = 0,
                        boardUrl = "custom://my_save",
                        description = "Custom board for saved items",
                        isCustom = true,
                        customDomains = "",
                        isDefault = false,
                        isSpecialNonDeletable = true
                    )
                )
            }
            val favorites = dao.getBoardById("board_favorites")
            if (favorites == null) {
                dao.insertBoard(
                    BoardEntity(
                        id = "board_favorites",
                        title = "Favorites",
                        slug = "favorites",
                        ownerUsername = "you",
                        ownerDisplayName = "Favorites",
                        ownerAvatarUrl = "",
                        pinCount = 0,
                        followerCount = 0,
                        boardUrl = "custom://favorites",
                        description = "Your favorite images",
                        isCustom = true,
                        customDomains = "",
                        isDefault = false,
                        isSpecialNonDeletable = true
                    )
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class, androidx.compose.foundation.ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
private fun FloatingShareDialog(
    sharedLinks: List<String>,
    sharedImageUris: List<Uri>,
    onDismissAndSaveDefault: () -> Unit,
    onSaveToBoards: (targetBoardIds: List<String>, extractMultiple: Boolean, putAlbum: Boolean, targetAlbumId: String?) -> Unit
) {
    val context = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext.current
    var boards by remember { mutableStateOf<List<BoardEntity>>(emptyList()) }
    var albums by remember { mutableStateOf<List<CustomAlbumEntity>>(emptyList()) }
    var isLoadingBoards by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var isSelectMode by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val selectedBoardIds = remember { mutableStateListOf<String>() }
    var selectedAlbumId by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    var extractMultipleImages by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var putIntoAlbum by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val dao = AppDatabase.getDatabase(context).boardDao()
            val list = dao.getAllBoards()
            boards = list
            albums = dao.getAllAlbums()
            val defaultB = list.firstOrNull { it.isDefault } ?: list.firstOrNull { it.id == "board_dump" } ?: list.firstOrNull { it.id == "board_my_save" } ?: list.firstOrNull()
            defaultB?.let { selectedBoardIds.add(it.id) }
            isLoadingBoards = false
        }
    }

    val itemCount = sharedLinks.size + sharedImageUris.size

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.55f))
            .clickable { onDismissAndSaveDefault() },
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Card(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.92f)
                .wrapContentHeight()
                .clickable(enabled = false) {}
                .testTag("floating_share_dialog_card"),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
            elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 12.dp)
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(20.dp)
            ) {
                // Header
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                ) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(36.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(PinterestRed.copy(alpha = 0.12f)),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Share,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text(
                                text = "Save to PinBoard",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                fontSize = 17.sp,
                                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = if (itemCount > 1) "$itemCount items detected" else "1 item detected",
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { isSelectMode = !isSelectMode },
                            modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Checklist,
                                contentDescription = "Toggle multi-select mode",
                                tint = if (isSelectMode) PinterestRed else PinterestTextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = onDismissAndSaveDefault,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Close,
                                contentDescription = "Close and Save to Default",
                                tint = PinterestTextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // Checklist 1: Add multiple main images from webpage (unchecked by default)
                if (sharedLinks.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clickable {
                                extractMultipleImages = !extractMultipleImages
                                if (!extractMultipleImages) putIntoAlbum = false
                            }
                            .padding(vertical = 4.dp)
                            .testTag("share_extract_multiple_checkbox")
                    ) {
                        Checkbox(
                            checked = extractMultipleImages,
                            onCheckedChange = {
                                extractMultipleImages = it
                                if (!it) putIntoAlbum = false
                            },
                            colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = PinterestRed)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text(
                                text = "Add multiple main images from webpage",
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Extract content images in order (first to last)",
                                fontSize = 10.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // Checklist 2: Shown when extractMultipleImages is checked
                    if (extractMultipleImages) {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(start = 24.dp)
                                .clickable { putIntoAlbum = !putIntoAlbum }
                                .padding(vertical = 4.dp)
                                .testTag("share_put_into_album_checkbox")
                        ) {
                            Checkbox(
                                checked = putIntoAlbum,
                                onCheckedChange = { putIntoAlbum = it },
                                colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = PinterestRed)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Column {
                                Text(
                                    text = "Put into album",
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "Save as an album inside the chosen board",
                                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                                    color = PinterestTextSecondary
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                Text(
                    text = if (isSelectMode) "Select multiple destination boards:" else "Choose destination board (press & hold for multi-select):",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                if (isLoadingBoards) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(100.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = PinterestRed, strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                    }
                } else {
                    FlowRow(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        boards.forEach { board ->
                            val isSelected = selectedBoardIds.contains(board.id) && selectedAlbumId == null
                            Surface(
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                color = if (isSelected) PinterestBlack else PinterestPillGray,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .combinedClickable(
                                        onClick = {
                                            selectedAlbumId = null
                                            if (isSelectMode) {
                                                if (isSelected) selectedBoardIds.remove(board.id)
                                                else selectedBoardIds.add(board.id)
                                            } else {
                                                selectedBoardIds.clear()
                                                selectedBoardIds.add(board.id)
                                                onSaveToBoards(listOf(board.id), extractMultipleImages, putIntoAlbum, null)
                                            }
                                        },
                                        onLongClick = {
                                            selectedAlbumId = null
                                            isSelectMode = true
                                            if (isSelected) selectedBoardIds.remove(board.id)
                                            else selectedBoardIds.add(board.id)
                                        }
                                    )
                                    .testTag("share_target_board_${board.id}")
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    if (isSelected) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                                            contentDescription = null,
                                            tint = Color.White,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                        )
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                    }
                                    Text(
                                        text = board.title,
                                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                        color = if (isSelected) Color.White else PinterestBlack,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    if (board.isDefault) {
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = "(Default)",
                                            fontSize = 10.sp,
                                            color = if (isSelected) Color.LightGray else PinterestTextSecondary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Also allow selecting existing non-sort albums
                        albums.forEach { album ->
                            val isAlbumSelected = selectedAlbumId == album.id
                            Surface(
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                color = if (isAlbumSelected) PinterestRed else PinterestPillGray,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clickable {
                                        selectedAlbumId = if (isAlbumSelected) null else album.id
                                    }
                                    .testTag("share_target_album_${album.id}")
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Folder,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = if (isAlbumSelected) Color.White else PinterestRed,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = album.title,
                                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                                        fontWeight = if (isAlbumSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                        color = if (isAlbumSelected) Color.White else PinterestBlack,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                // Bottom actions
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "Exiting will save to default board",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                    )

                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            if (selectedAlbumId != null) {
                                onSaveToBoards(emptyList(), extractMultipleImages, putIntoAlbum, selectedAlbumId)
                            } else if (selectedBoardIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                                onSaveToBoards(selectedBoardIds.toList(), extractMultipleImages, putIntoAlbum, null)
                            } else {
                                onDismissAndSaveDefault()
                            }
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("share_dialog_save_btn")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = if (putIntoAlbum) "Save as Album"
                            else if (selectedAlbumId != null) "Add to Album"
                            else if (selectedBoardIds.size > 1) "Save to ${selectedBoardIds.size} Boards"
                            else "Save",
                            color = Color.White,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


--- FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\util\BackupManager.kt ---
package com.example.util

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Environment
import com.example.data.local.AppDatabase
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

object BackupManager {

    private const val PREFS_NAME = "pinboard_backup_prefs"
    private const val KEY_BACKUP_ENABLED = "backup_enabled"
    private const val KEY_BACKUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES = "backup_interval_minutes"
    private const val KEY_MAX_BACKUP_FILES = "max_backup_files"
    private const val KEY_LAST_BACKUP_TIME = "last_backup_time"
    const val DEFAULT_MAX_BACKUP_FILES = 5

    val MAX_FILES_OPTIONS: List<Int> = listOf(1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50)

    val INTERVAL_OPTIONS: List<Pair<String, Long>> = listOf(
        "1 hour" to 60L,
        "3 hours" to 180L,
        "1 day" to 1440L,
        "3 days" to 4320L,
        "1 week" to 10080L,
        "2 weeks" to 20160L,
        "1 month" to 43200L,
        "3 months" to 129600L,
        "6 months" to 259200L,
        "1 year" to 525600L
    )

    fun isBackupEnabled(context: Context): Boolean {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_BACKUP_ENABLED, true)
    }

    fun setBackupEnabled(context: Context, enabled: Boolean) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_BACKUP_ENABLED, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun getBackupIntervalMinutes(context: Context): Long {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getLong(KEY_BACKUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES, 1440L) // default 1 day
    }

    fun setBackupIntervalMinutes(context: Context, minutes: Long) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().putLong(KEY_BACKUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES, minutes).apply()
    }

    fun getMaxBackupFiles(context: Context): Int {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_MAX_BACKUP_FILES, DEFAULT_MAX_BACKUP_FILES)
    }

    fun setMaxBackupFiles(context: Context, maxFiles: Int) {
        val clamped = maxFiles.coerceAtLeast(1)
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_MAX_BACKUP_FILES, clamped).apply()
        try {
            val dir = getBackupDirectory(context)
            pruneOldBackups(dir, clamped)
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun getBackupDirectory(context: Context): File {
        val downloadDir = try {
            Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
        val targetDir = if (downloadDir != null) {
            File(downloadDir, "PinterestViewerDownloads/Backup")
        } else {
            File(context.filesDir, "Download/PinterestViewerDownloads/Backup")
        }

        if (!targetDir.exists()) {
            targetDir.mkdirs()
        }

        if (targetDir.canWrite()) {
            return targetDir
        }

        val fallbackExternal = File(context.getExternalFilesDir(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS), "PinterestViewerDownloads/Backup").apply {
            if (!exists()) mkdirs()
        }
        if (fallbackExternal.canWrite()) {
            return fallbackExternal
        }

        // Clean up legacy internal filesDir backups to keep user data compact
        try {
            File(context.filesDir, "Download/PinterestViewerDownloads/Backup").takeIf { it.exists() }?.deleteRecursively()
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}

        // Store non-permanent backup data in cacheDir to keep User Data as tiny as possible
        val internalDir = File(context.cacheDir, "PinterestViewerDownloads/Backup").apply {
            if (!exists()) mkdirs()
        }
        return internalDir
    }

    fun getLastBackupTime(context: Context): Long {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getLong(KEY_LAST_BACKUP_TIME, 0L)
    }

    private fun setLastBackupTime(context: Context, time: Long) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().putLong(KEY_LAST_BACKUP_TIME, time).apply()
    }

    fun checkAndPerformScheduledBackup(context: Context) {
        if (!isBackupEnabled(context)) return
        val intervalMinutes = getBackupIntervalMinutes(context)
        val lastBackup = getLastBackupTime(context)
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val intervalMillis = intervalMinutes * 60 * 1000L

        if (now - lastBackup >= intervalMillis) {
            performBackupAsync(context)
        }
    }

    fun performBackupAsync(context: Context) {
        kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO).launch {
            try {
                performBackup(context)
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                t.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun performBackup(context: Context): Result<File> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val db = AppDatabase.getDatabase(context)
            val dao = db.boardDao()

            val boards = dao.getAllBoards()
            val allPins = dao.getAllPins()

            val rootJson = JSONObject()
            rootJson.put("version", 1)
            rootJson.put("timestamp", System.currentTimeMillis())
            rootJson.put("created_at", SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss'Z'", Locale.US).format(Date()))

            // Export all SharedPreferences settings
            val settingsObj = JSONObject()
            val appSettingsPrefs = context.getSharedPreferences("app_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            appSettingsPrefs.all.forEach { (k, v) ->
                settingsObj.put(k, v ?: JSONObject.NULL)
            }
            rootJson.put("settings", settingsObj)

            // Export board history
            val historyPrefs = context.getSharedPreferences("pinterest_board_history", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            val historyJsonStr = historyPrefs.getString("history_json", null)
            if (historyJsonStr != null) {
                try {
                    rootJson.put("board_history", JSONArray(historyJsonStr))
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    rootJson.put("board_history", historyJsonStr)
                }
            }

            // Export boards
            val boardsArray = JSONArray()
            boards.forEach { b ->
                val bObj = JSONObject().apply {
                    put("id", b.id)
                    put("title", b.title)
                    put("slug", b.slug)
                    put("ownerUsername", b.ownerUsername)
                    put("ownerDisplayName", b.ownerDisplayName)
                    put("ownerAvatarUrl", b.ownerAvatarUrl)
                    put("pinCount", b.pinCount)
                    put("followerCount", b.followerCount)
                    put("boardUrl", b.boardUrl)
                    put("description", b.description)
                    put("isCustom", b.isCustom)
                    put("customDomains", b.customDomains)
                    put("isDefault", b.isDefault)
                    put("isSpecialNonDeletable", b.isSpecialNonDeletable)
                    put("lastUpdatedMillis", b.lastUpdatedMillis)
                }
                boardsArray.put(bObj)
            }
            rootJson.put("boards", boardsArray)

            // Save to /Download/PinterestViewerDownloads
            val chosenBackupDir = getBackupDirectory(context)

            // Export pins metadata and info (excluding raw image binary data)
            // Requirement 1: Make sure to not delete data even if image itself is deleted from the app
            val archivedPinsMap = linkedMapOf<String, JSONObject>()

            // Load from existing master archive if available
            val archiveFile = File(chosenBackupDir, "master_pins_archive.json")
            if (archiveFile.exists()) {
                try {
                    val existingArchiveStr = archiveFile.readText()
                    val existingArray = JSONArray(existingArchiveStr)
                    for (i in 0 until existingArray.length()) {
                        val obj = existingArray.getJSONObject(i)
                        val key = obj.optString("id").ifBlank { obj.optString("imageUrl") }
                        if (key.isNotBlank()) {
                            archivedPinsMap[key] = obj
                        }
                    }
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }

            // Merge with all pins currently in database
            allPins.forEach { p ->
                val pObj = JSONObject().apply {
                    put("id", p.id)
                    put("boardId", p.boardId)
                    put("title", p.title)
                    put("description", p.description)
                    put("imageUrl", p.imageUrl)
                    put("thumbnailUrl", p.thumbnailUrl)
                    put("author", p.author)
                    put("boardName", p.boardName)
                    put("sourceUrl", p.sourceUrl)
                    put("width", p.width)
                    put("height", p.height)
                    put("dominantColor", p.dominantColor)
                    put("dateAddedMillis", p.dateAddedMillis)
                    put("savesCount", p.savesCount)
                    put("likesCount", p.likesCount)
                    put("fileSizeKb", p.fileSizeKb)
                    put("orderIndex", p.orderIndex)
                    put("boardPosition", p.boardPosition)
                    put("firstSeenAt", p.firstSeenAt)
                    put("firstSeenAtMillis", p.firstSeenAtMillis)
                    put("pinterestCreatedAt", p.pinterestCreatedAt ?: "")
                    put("mimeType", p.mimeType)
                    put("sourceDomain", p.sourceDomain)
                    put("isBrokenOrUnreachable", p.isBrokenOrUnreachable)
                }
                val key = p.id.ifBlank { p.imageUrl }
                archivedPinsMap[key] = pObj
            }

            val pinsArray = JSONArray()
            archivedPinsMap.values.forEach { pinsArray.put(it) }
            rootJson.put("pins", pinsArray)

            try {
                archiveFile.writeText(pinsArray.toString())
            } catch (_: Exception) {}

            val timestampStr = SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss", Locale.US).format(Date())
            val backupFileName = "pinboard_backup_$timestampStr.json"
            val targetFile = File(chosenBackupDir, backupFileName)

            // Always write latest backup to target and latest.json
            val jsonString = rootJson.toString()
            targetFile.writeText(jsonString)

            val latestFile = File(chosenBackupDir, "latest_backup.json")
            try {
                latestFile.writeText(jsonString)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}

            // Prune old backups to respect max backup files limit (Requirement 3)
            pruneOldBackups(chosenBackupDir, getMaxBackupFiles(context))

            setLastBackupTime(context, System.currentTimeMillis())
            Result.success(targetFile)
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            t.printStackTrace()
            Result.failure(t)
        }
    }

    private fun pruneOldBackups(backupDir: File, maxCount: Int) {
        try {
            val backupFiles = backupDir.listFiles { file ->
                file.isFile && file.name.startsWith("pinboard_backup_") && file.name.endsWith(".json")
            } ?: return

            if (backupFiles.size > maxCount) {
                // Sort by last modified descending (newest first)
                val sorted = backupFiles.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }
                // Delete everything past maxCount
                for (i in maxCount until sorted.size) {
                    try {
                        sorted[i].delete()
                    } catch (t: Throwable) {
                        t.printStackTrace()
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            t.printStackTrace()
        }
    }

    fun getAvailableBackups(context: Context): List<File> {
        val primaryDir = getBackupDirectory(context)
        val files = mutableListOf<File>()

        fun collectFrom(dir: File) {
            if (dir.exists() && dir.isDirectory) {
                dir.listFiles { f ->
                    f.isFile && f.extension.equals("json", ignoreCase = true) &&
                            (f.name.startsWith("pinboard_backup_") || f.name == "latest_backup.json")
                }?.let { files.addAll(it) }
            }
        }

        collectFrom(primaryDir)
        // Also check parent folder in case previous backups were placed there
        primaryDir.parentFile?.let { parent ->
            if (parent.name == "PinterestViewerDownloads") {
                collectFrom(parent)
            }
        }

        return files.distinctBy { it.absolutePath }
            .sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }
    }

    data class RestoreSummary(
        val boardsCount: Int,
        val pinsCount: Int,
        val settingsRestored: Boolean
    )

    suspend fun restoreBackup(context: Context, backupFile: File): Result<RestoreSummary> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            if (!backupFile.exists() || !backupFile.canRead()) {
                return@withContext Result.failure(Exception("Backup file does not exist or cannot be read"))
            }

            val jsonStr = backupFile.readText()
            val rootJson = JSONObject(jsonStr)

            val db = AppDatabase.getDatabase(context)
            val dao = db.boardDao()

            var restoredBoardsCount = 0
            var restoredPinsCount = 0
            var settingsRestored = false

            // 1. Restore Boards
            val boardsArray = rootJson.optJSONArray("boards")
            if (boardsArray != null && boardsArray.length() > 0) {
                val boardEntities = mutableListOf<com.example.data.local.BoardEntity>()
                for (i in 0 until boardsArray.length()) {
                    val bObj = boardsArray.getJSONObject(i)
                    val id = bObj.optString("id")
                    if (id.isNotBlank()) {
                        boardEntities.add(
                            com.example.data.local.BoardEntity(
                                id = id,
                                title = bObj.optString("title", ""),
                                slug = bObj.optString("slug", ""),
                                ownerUsername = bObj.optString("ownerUsername", ""),
                                ownerDisplayName = bObj.optString("ownerDisplayName", ""),
                                ownerAvatarUrl = bObj.optString("ownerAvatarUrl", ""),
                                pinCount = bObj.optInt("pinCount", 0),
                                followerCount = bObj.optInt("followerCount", 0),
                                boardUrl = bObj.optString("boardUrl", ""),
                                description = bObj.optString("description", ""),
                                isCustom = bObj.optBoolean("isCustom", false),
                                customDomains = bObj.optString("customDomains", ""),
                                isDefault = bObj.optBoolean("isDefault", false),
                                isSpecialNonDeletable = bObj.optBoolean("isSpecialNonDeletable", false),
                                lastUpdatedMillis = bObj.optLong("lastUpdatedMillis", System.currentTimeMillis())
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (boardEntities.isNotEmpty()) {
                    dao.insertBoards(boardEntities)
                    restoredBoardsCount = boardEntities.size
                }
            }

            // 2. Restore Pins
            val pinsArray = rootJson.optJSONArray("pins")
            if (pinsArray != null && pinsArray.length() > 0) {
                val pinEntities = mutableListOf<com.example.data.local.PinEntity>()
                for (i in 0 until pinsArray.length()) {
                    val pObj = pinsArray.getJSONObject(i)
                    val id = pObj.optString("id")
                    val boardId = pObj.optString("boardId")
                    val imageUrl = pObj.optString("imageUrl")
                    if (id.isNotBlank() && imageUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                        pinEntities.add(
                            com.example.data.local.PinEntity(
                                id = id,
                                boardId = boardId.ifBlank { "board_dump" },
                                title = pObj.optString("title", ""),
                                description = pObj.optString("description", ""),
                                imageUrl = imageUrl,
                                thumbnailUrl = pObj.optString("thumbnailUrl", imageUrl),
                                author = pObj.optString("author", ""),
                                boardName = pObj.optString("boardName", ""),
                                sourceUrl = pObj.optString("sourceUrl", ""),
                                width = pObj.optInt("width", 0),
                                height = pObj.optInt("height", 0),
                                dominantColor = pObj.optLong("dominantColor", 0xFF333333L),
                                dateAddedMillis = pObj.optLong("dateAddedMillis", System.currentTimeMillis()),
                                savesCount = pObj.optInt("savesCount", 0),
                                likesCount = pObj.optInt("likesCount", 0),
                                fileSizeKb = pObj.optLong("fileSizeKb", 0L),
                                orderIndex = pObj.optInt("orderIndex", i),
                                boardPosition = pObj.optInt("boardPosition", i),
                                firstSeenAt = pObj.optString("firstSeenAt", ""),
                                firstSeenAtMillis = pObj.optLong("firstSeenAtMillis", 0L),
                                pinterestCreatedAt = pObj.optString("pinterestCreatedAt", "").ifBlank { null },
                                mimeType = pObj.optString("mimeType", "image/jpeg"),
                                sourceDomain = pObj.optString("sourceDomain", ""),
                                isBrokenOrUnreachable = pObj.optBoolean("isBrokenOrUnreachable", false)
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (pinEntities.isNotEmpty()) {
                    dao.upsertPinsSafely(pinEntities)
                    restoredPinsCount = pinEntities.size
                }
            }

            // 3. Restore App Settings SharedPreferences
            val settingsObj = rootJson.optJSONObject("settings")
            if (settingsObj != null) {
                val editor = context.getSharedPreferences("app_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
                val keys = settingsObj.keys()
                while (keys.hasNext()) {
                    val key = keys.next()
                    when (val value = settingsObj.get(key)) {
                        is Boolean -> editor.putBoolean(key, value)
                        is Int -> editor.putInt(key, value)
                        is Long -> editor.putLong(key, value)
                        is Float -> editor.putFloat(key, value)
                        is Double -> editor.putFloat(key, value.toFloat())
                        is String -> editor.putString(key, value)
                        is JSONArray -> {
                            val set = mutableSetOf<String>()
                            for (j in 0 until value.length()) {
                                set.add(value.getString(j))
                            }
                            editor.putStringSet(key, set)
                        }
                    }
                }
                editor.apply()
                settingsRestored = true
            }

            // 4. Restore Board History
            val historyVal = rootJson.opt("board_history")
            if (historyVal != null) {
                val historyPrefs = context.getSharedPreferences("pinterest_board_history", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
                historyPrefs.edit().putString("history_json", historyVal.toString()).apply()
            }

            Result.success(RestoreSummary(restoredBoardsCount, restoredPinsCount, settingsRestored))
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            t.printStackTrace()
            Result.failure(t)
        }
    }
}


--- FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\util\CrashMonitor.kt ---
package com.example.util

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import java.io.File
import java.io.PrintWriter
import java.io.StringWriter
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue

/**
 * Global Crash Monitor and Diagnostics Logging.
 * Captures, stores, and exposes crashes and error stacktraces
 * so users and developers can inspect the exact cause of any error.
 */
object CrashMonitor {
    private const val TAG = "CrashMonitor"
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "crash_monitor_prefs"
    private const val KEY_LAST_CRASH = "last_crash_trace"
    private const val KEY_LAST_CRASH_TIME = "last_crash_time"

    private val breadcrumbs = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<String>()
    private var defaultHandler: Thread.UncaughtExceptionHandler? = null
    private var isInitialized = false
    private var applicationContext: Context? = null

    private val _lastCrashState = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val lastCrashState: StateFlow<String?> = _lastCrashState.asStateFlow()

    fun init(context: Context) {
        if (isInitialized) return
        isInitialized = true
        applicationContext = context.applicationContext

        // Check if there is a previous crash stored
        val previous = getLastCrash(context)
        if (!previous.isNullOrBlank()) {
            _lastCrashState.value = previous
            Log.e(TAG, "Loaded previous crash log:\n$previous")
        }

        defaultHandler = Thread.getDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler()
        Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler { thread, throwable ->
            handleUncaughtException(thread, throwable)
        }
    }

    fun addBreadcrumb(message: String) {
        val time = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss.SSS", Locale.US).format(Date())
        val entry = "[$time] $message"
        breadcrumbs.add(entry)
        while (breadcrumbs.size > 40) {
            breadcrumbs.poll()
        }
        try {
            Log.d(TAG, "Breadcrumb: $entry")
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    private fun isCancellation(throwable: Throwable?): Boolean {
        var curr = throwable
        while (curr != null) {
            val name = curr.javaClass.name
            if (curr is java.util.concurrent.CancellationException ||
                curr is kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException ||
                name.contains("CancellationException", ignoreCase = true) ||
                name.contains("JobCancellationException", ignoreCase = true)
            ) {
                return true
            }
            curr = curr.cause
        }
        return false
    }

    fun recordException(throwable: Throwable, contextTag: String) {
        if (isCancellation(throwable)) {
            return
        }
        val ctx = applicationContext ?: return
        val trace = buildCrashReport(Thread.currentThread().name, throwable, contextTag)
        saveCrashReport(ctx, trace)
        _lastCrashState.value = trace
        try {
            Log.e(TAG, "Non-fatal exception recorded [$contextTag]:", throwable)
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    private fun handleUncaughtException(thread: Thread, throwable: Throwable) {
        if (isCancellation(throwable)) {
            return
        }
        val ctx = applicationContext
        val report = buildCrashReport(thread.name, throwable, "Uncaught Exception")
        Log.e(TAG, "FATAL CRASH DETECTED on thread ${thread.name}:\n$report", throwable)

        if (ctx != null) {
            saveCrashReport(ctx, report)
            try {
                val intent = android.content.Intent().apply {
                    setClassName(ctx.packageName, "com.example.ui.CrashReportActivity")
                    putExtra("extra_crash_report", report)
                    addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK)
                }
                ctx.startActivity(intent)
                Process.killProcess(Process.myPid())
                System.exit(10)
                return
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed to launch CrashReportActivity", t)
            }
        }

        _lastCrashState.value = report

        // Forward to default handler so OS/Android knows about it
        defaultHandler?.uncaughtException(thread, throwable) ?: Process.killProcess(Process.myPid())
    }

    private fun buildCrashReport(threadName: String, throwable: Throwable, contextTag: String): String {
        val sw = StringWriter()
        val pw = PrintWriter(sw)
        throwable.printStackTrace(pw)
        val stackTraceString = sw.toString()

        val timeStr = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS", Locale.US).format(Date())
        val breadcrumbText = breadcrumbs.toList().joinToString("\n  ")

        return buildString {
            appendLine("=== PINBOARD CRASH / ERROR REPORT ===")
            appendLine("Time: $timeStr")
            appendLine("Tag: $contextTag")
            appendLine("Thread: $threadName (ID: ${Thread.currentThread().id})")
            appendLine("Device: ${Build.MANUFACTURER} ${Build.MODEL} (Android ${Build.VERSION.RELEASE}, API ${Build.VERSION.SDK_INT})")
            appendLine("Exception: ${throwable.javaClass.name}")
            appendLine("Message: ${throwable.message ?: "None"}")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("--- BREADCRUMBS ---")
            if (breadcrumbText.isNotBlank()) {
                appendLine("  $breadcrumbText")
            } else {
                appendLine("  (No breadcrumbs recorded)")
            }
            appendLine()
            appendLine("--- STACK TRACE ---")
            appendLine(stackTraceString)
            appendLine("=====================================")
        }
    }

    private fun saveCrashReport(context: Context, report: String) {
        try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            prefs.edit()
                .putString(KEY_LAST_CRASH, report)
                .putLong(KEY_LAST_CRASH_TIME, System.currentTimeMillis())
                .commit() // Use commit to ensure it's written immediately before process exit

            val file = File(context.cacheDir, "last_crash_log.txt")
            file.writeText(report)
            // Clean up legacy location in filesDir to keep user data compact
            try { File(context.filesDir, "last_crash_log.txt").delete() } catch (_: Throwable) {}
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to persist crash report", e)
        }
    }

    fun getLastCrash(context: Context): String? {
        return try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            val fromPrefs = prefs.getString(KEY_LAST_CRASH, null)
            if (!fromPrefs.isNullOrBlank()) return fromPrefs

            val file = File(context.cacheDir, "last_crash_log.txt")
            if (file.exists()) file.readText() else {
                val legacy = File(context.filesDir, "last_crash_log.txt")
                if (legacy.exists()) legacy.readText() else null
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    fun clearLastCrash(context: Context) {
        try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            prefs.edit().remove(KEY_LAST_CRASH).remove(KEY_LAST_CRASH_TIME).apply()
            val file = File(context.cacheDir, "last_crash_log.txt")
            if (file.exists()) file.delete()
            val legacy = File(context.filesDir, "last_crash_log.txt")
            if (legacy.exists()) legacy.delete()
            _lastCrashState.value = null
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to clear crash log", e)
        }
    }
}


--- FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\util\Downloader.kt ---
package com.example.util

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.media.MediaScannerConnection
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Environment
import android.provider.MediaStore
import com.example.model.PinItem
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

data class DownloadProgress(
    val current: Int,
    val total: Int,
    val currentPinTitle: String,
    val isComplete: Boolean = false,
    val destinationFolder: String = "Download/PinterestViewerDownloads",
    val error: String? = null
)

object Downloader {
    private val client = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .followRedirects(true)
        .build()

    const val FOLDER_NAME = "PinterestViewerDownloads"

    suspend fun downloadPins(
        context: Context,
        pins: List<PinItem>,
        onProgress: (DownloadProgress) -> Unit
    ): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        var successCount = 0
        val total = pins.size

        pins.forEachIndexed { index, pin ->
            onProgress(
                DownloadProgress(
                    current = index + 1,
                    total = total,
                    currentPinTitle = pin.title.ifBlank { "Pin #${pin.id}" }
                )
            )

            val success = downloadSinglePinInternal(context, pin)
            if (success) {
                successCount++
            }
        }

        onProgress(
            DownloadProgress(
                current = total,
                total = total,
                currentPinTitle = "Done",
                isComplete = true
            )
        )

        successCount
    }

    suspend fun downloadSinglePin(context: Context, pin: PinItem): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        downloadSinglePinInternal(context, pin)
    }

    private fun downloadSinglePinInternal(context: Context, pin: PinItem): Boolean {
        try {
            val sanitizedTitle = pin.title
                .take(30)
                .replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]"), "_")
                .trim('_')
                .ifBlank { "pin" }
            val fileName = "Pinterest_${sanitizedTitle}_${pin.id}.jpg"
            val mimeType = "image/jpeg"

            val candidateUrls = listOf(pin.imageUrl, pin.thumbnailUrl).filter { it.isNotBlank() }.distinct()
            var responseBody: okhttp3.ResponseBody? = null
            for (url in candidateUrls) {
                try {
                    val request = Request.Builder()
                        .url(url)
                        .header("User-Agent", "Mozilla/5.0 (Android; Mobile; rv:109.0)")
                        .build()
                    val response = client.newCall(request).execute()
                    if (response.isSuccessful && response.body != null) {
                        responseBody = response.body
                        break
                    }
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
            if (responseBody == null) return false

            val inputStream: InputStream = responseBody.byteStream()

            return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                // Android 10+ MediaStore Downloads or Images
                val values = ContentValues().apply {
                    put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, fileName)
                    put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, mimeType)
                    put(
                        MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH,
                        "${Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS}/$FOLDER_NAME"
                    )
                    put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 1)
                }

                val resolver = context.contentResolver
                // Use MediaStore.Downloads on API 29+
                val uri: Uri? = resolver.insert(MediaStore.Downloads.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, values)
                    ?: resolver.insert(MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, values)

                if (uri != null) {
                    resolver.openOutputStream(uri)?.use { outputStream: OutputStream ->
                        inputStream.copyTo(outputStream)
                    }
                    values.clear()
                    values.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 0)
                    resolver.update(uri, values, null, null)
                    true
                } else {
                    false
                }
            } else {
                // Android 9 and below
                val downloadsDir = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS)
                val targetDir = File(downloadsDir, FOLDER_NAME)
                if (!targetDir.exists()) {
                    targetDir.mkdirs()
                }
                val outputFile = File(targetDir, fileName)
                FileOutputStream(outputFile).use { outputStream ->
                    inputStream.copyTo(outputStream)
                }
                MediaScannerConnection.scanFile(
                    context,
                    arrayOf(outputFile.absolutePath),
                    arrayOf(mimeType),
                    null
                )
                true
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            return false
        }
    }
}


--- FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\util\ImageCacheManager.kt ---
package com.example.util

import android.content.Context
import coil.Coil
import coil.ImageLoader
import coil.disk.DiskCache
import coil.memory.MemoryCache
import java.io.File

object ImageCacheManager {
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "image_cache_prefs"
    private const val KEY_MAX_DISK_CACHE_MB = "max_disk_cache_mb"
    const val DEFAULT_MAX_DISK_CACHE_MB = 512
    const val MIN_CACHE_MB = 50
    const val MAX_CACHE_MB = 4096

    fun getMaxDiskCacheMb(context: Context): Int {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_MAX_DISK_CACHE_MB, DEFAULT_MAX_DISK_CACHE_MB)
    }

    fun setMaxDiskCacheMb(context: Context, maxMb: Int) {
        val clamped = maxMb.coerceIn(MIN_CACHE_MB, MAX_CACHE_MB)
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .putInt(KEY_MAX_DISK_CACHE_MB, clamped)
            .apply()

        applyImageLoader(context, clamped)
    }

    fun applyImageLoader(context: Context, maxMb: Int = getMaxDiskCacheMb(context)) {
        val cacheDirectory = context.cacheDir.resolve("image_cache")
        val imageLoader = ImageLoader.Builder(context)
            .diskCache {
                DiskCache.Builder()
                    .directory(cacheDirectory)
                    .maxSizeBytes(maxMb.toLong() * 1024L * 1024L)
                    .build()
            }
            .memoryCache {
                MemoryCache.Builder(context)
                    .maxSizePercent(0.25)
                    .build()
            }
            .crossfade(true)
            .build()

        Coil.setImageLoader(imageLoader)
    }

    fun getDiskCacheUsageBytes(context: Context): Long {
        val cacheDir = context.cacheDir.resolve("image_cache")
        if (!cacheDir.exists()) return 0L
        return getDirSize(cacheDir)
    }

    fun formatBytes(bytes: Long): String {
        if (bytes <= 0) return "0 MB"
        val mb = bytes.toDouble() / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
        return if (mb < 0.1) {
            val kb = bytes / 1024
            "$kb KB"
        } else {
            String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1f MB", mb)
        }
    }

    private fun getDirSize(dir: File): Long {
        var size = 0L
        dir.listFiles()?.forEach { file ->
            size += if (file.isDirectory) getDirSize(file) else file.length()
        }
        return size
    }

    fun clearCache(context: Context) {
        try {
            Coil.imageLoader(context).diskCache?.clear()
            Coil.imageLoader(context).memoryCache?.clear()
            val cacheDir = context.cacheDir.resolve("image_cache")
            if (cacheDir.exists()) {
                cacheDir.deleteRecursively()
            }
            cleanupNonPermanentUserData(context)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
        applyImageLoader(context)
    }

    fun cleanupNonPermanentUserData(context: Context) {
        try {
            // Clean up WebView disk caches that Chromium puts in app_webview dataDir (which inflate "User data")
            val dataDir = context.applicationInfo.dataDir?.let { java.io.File(it) } ?: context.filesDir.parentFile
            dataDir?.listFiles()?.forEach { file ->
                if (file.name.startsWith("app_webview")) {
                    val subDirs = listOf("Cache", "GPUCache", "Code Cache", "Media Cache", "blob_storage", "Service Worker")
                    subDirs.forEach { sub ->
                        try {
                            java.io.File(file, sub).takeIf { it.exists() }?.deleteRecursively()
                            java.io.File(file, "Default/$sub").takeIf { it.exists() }?.deleteRecursively()
                        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
                    }
                }
            }

            // Clean temporary files in filesDir
            try {
                java.io.File(context.filesDir, "temp").takeIf { it.exists() }?.deleteRecursively()
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // Truncate SQLite WAL file to minimize Room database size on disk
            com.example.data.local.AppDatabase.optimizeAndShrinkDatabase(context)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
    }
}


--- FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\util\ImageLinkResolver.kt ---
package com.example.util

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import com.example.model.PinItem
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.net.URI
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * Data class representing an extracted image candidate for scoring.
 * Based on the two-pass browserless extraction architecture.
 */
data class ImageCandidate(
    val url: String,
    var source: String,
    var score: Float = 0f,
    var preliminaryScore: Float = 0f,
    var width: Int? = null,
    var height: Int? = null,
    var alt: String = "",
    var context: String = "",
    val sources: MutableSet<String> = mutableSetOf(source)
)

object ImageLinkResolver {
    private val client: OkHttpClient by lazy {
        OkHttpClient.Builder()
            .connectTimeout(12, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .readTimeout(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .followRedirects(true)
            .followSslRedirects(true)
            .build()
    }

    private const val USER_AGENT =
        "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 14; Mobile) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36"

    private const val TOP_N_CANDIDATES = 5
    private const val MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 512 * 1024 // 512 KB

    // Regexes for HTML parsing
    private val META_TAG_REGEX = Regex("""<meta\b[^>]*>""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val SCRIPT_TAG_REGEX = Regex("""<script\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)</script>""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val IMG_TAG_REGEX = Regex("""<img\b[^>]*>""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val SOURCE_TAG_REGEX = Regex("""<source\b[^>]*>""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val ATTR_REGEX = Regex("""([a-zA-Z0-9_:-]+)\s*=\s*(?:"([^"]*)"|'([^']*)'|([^\s>]+))""")

    // Title Tag Regexes
    private val OG_TITLE_REGEX = Regex("""<meta[^>]+property=["']og:title["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val TWITTER_TITLE_REGEX = Regex("""<meta[^>]+name=["']twitter:title["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val TITLE_TAG_REGEX = Regex("""<title[^>]*>([^<]+)</title>""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)

    // Image Board Webpage patterns (4chan, Boorus, opened image viewers)
    private val FOURCHAN_FILE_REGEX = Regex("""<div[^>]+class=["']fileText["'][^>]*>[\s\S]*?<a[^>]+href=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val FOURCHAN_THUMB_REGEX = Regex("""<a[^>]+class=["']fileThumb["'][^>]+href=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val BOORU_IMAGE_ID_REGEX = Regex("""<img[^>]+id=["']image["'][^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val BOORU_IMAGE_ID_ALT_REGEX = Regex("""<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["'][^>]+id=["']image["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val BOORU_CONTAINER_REGEX = Regex("""data-file-url=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val ORIGINAL_FILE_LINK_REGEX = Regex("""<a[^>]+class=["'][^"']*original-file[^"']*["'][^>]+href=["']([^"']+)["']""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)

    /**
     * Resolves a URL into a PinItem.
     * Automatically decides whether the link is an image link or webpage:
     * - If image link: uses direct image URL, determines dimensions & size.
     * - If webpage: runs browserless two-pass main image extraction (candidates -> preliminary scoring -> top 5 dimension check -> final scoring).
     * Saves original webpage link in sourceUrl, and actual image URL in imageUrl.
     */
    suspend fun resolveLink(url: String, boardName: String = "Custom Board"): PinItem = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val cleanUrl = url.trim()
        val domain = LinkExtractor.extractHost(cleanUrl)
        val defaultTitle = LinkExtractor.extractTitleFromUrl(cleanUrl)
        val uniqueId = generateDeterministicPinId(cleanUrl)

        var finalImageUrl = cleanUrl
        var finalTitle = defaultTitle
        var mimeType = guessMimeType(cleanUrl)
        var fileSizeKb = 250L
        var isBroken = false
        var imageWidth = 1080
        var imageHeight = 1350
        val sourceWebpageUrl = cleanUrl

        try {
            val request = Request.Builder()
                .url(cleanUrl)
                .header("User-Agent", USER_AGENT)
                .header("Accept", "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,image/avif,image/webp,image/apng,image/*,*/*;q=0.8")
                .header("Accept-Language", "en-US,en;q=0.9")
                .build()

            client.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
                if (!response.isSuccessful) {
                    isBroken = true
                } else {
                    val effectiveUrl = response.request.url.toString()
                    val contentType = response.header("Content-Type")?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) ?: ""
                    val contentLength = response.header("Content-Length")?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                    if (contentLength > 0) {
                        fileSizeKb = (contentLength / 1024L).coerceAtLeast(1L)
                    }

                    val isDirectImage = contentType.startsWith("image/")

                    if (isDirectImage) {
                        // Directly an image link
                        mimeType = contentType.substringBefore(";")
                        finalImageUrl = effectiveUrl

                        // Read dimensions if available
                        val dims = getImageDimensions(effectiveUrl)
                        if (dims != null) {
                            imageWidth = dims.first
                            imageHeight = dims.second
                        }
                    } else {
                        // Webpage link! Read HTML up to 512KB to extract main header / lead / board image
                        val htmlBody = response.body?.source()?.let { source ->
                            source.request(524288) // 512 KB
                            val buffer = source.buffer
                            val maxBytes = minOf(524288L, buffer.size)
                            buffer.clone().readUtf8(maxBytes)
                        } ?: ""

                        // Extract Page Title
                        val extractedTitle = extractTitleFromHtml(htmlBody)
                        if (extractedTitle.isNotBlank()) {
                            finalTitle = extractedTitle
                        }

                        // Run browserless two-pass main image extractor
                        val bestCandidate = extractMainImageTwoPass(htmlBody, effectiveUrl)
                        if (bestCandidate != null && bestCandidate.url.isNotBlank()) {
                            finalImageUrl = bestCandidate.url
                            mimeType = guessMimeType(finalImageUrl)
                            if (bestCandidate.width != null && bestCandidate.height != null) {
                                imageWidth = bestCandidate.width!!
                                imageHeight = bestCandidate.height!!
                            }

                            // Quick check for Content-Length and real Content-Type
                            try {
                                val imgCheckRequest = Request.Builder()
                                    .url(finalImageUrl)
                                    .header("User-Agent", USER_AGENT)
                                    .header("Referer", effectiveUrl)
                                    .build()

                                client.newCall(imgCheckRequest).execute().use { imgResp ->
                                    if (imgResp.isSuccessful) {
                                        val imgType = imgResp.header("Content-Type")?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) ?: ""
                                        if (imgType.startsWith("image/")) {
                                            mimeType = imgType.substringBefore(";")
                                        }
                                        val imgLen = imgResp.header("Content-Length")?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                                        if (imgLen > 0) {
                                            fileSizeKb = (imgLen / 1024L).coerceAtLeast(1L)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            } catch (_: Exception) {}
                        } else {
                            // No image could be identified
                            isBroken = true
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            isBroken = true
        }

        PinItem(
            id = uniqueId,
            title = finalTitle,
            description = if (sourceWebpageUrl != finalImageUrl) "Imported from $domain" else "",
            imageUrl = finalImageUrl,
            thumbnailUrl = finalImageUrl,
            author = domain.ifBlank { "Web Source" },
            boardName = boardName,
            sourceUrl = sourceWebpageUrl,
            width = imageWidth,
            height = imageHeight,
            dominantColor = 0xFF2D2D2DL,
            dateAddedMillis = System.currentTimeMillis(),
            fileSizeKb = fileSizeKb,
            mimeType = mimeType,
            sourceDomain = domain,
            isBrokenOrUnreachable = isBroken
        )
    }

    /**
     * Resolves multiple main images from a webpage URL in document order (first main image first, last image last).
     * If the URL is directly an image, returns a single-item list.
     */
    suspend fun resolveMultipleLinks(url: String, boardName: String = "Custom Board"): List<PinItem> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val cleanUrl = url.trim()
        val domain = LinkExtractor.extractHost(cleanUrl)
        val defaultTitle = LinkExtractor.extractTitleFromUrl(cleanUrl)

        try {
            val request = Request.Builder()
                .url(cleanUrl)
                .header("User-Agent", USER_AGENT)
                .header("Accept", "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,image/avif,image/webp,image/apng,image/*,*/*;q=0.8")
                .header("Accept-Language", "en-US,en;q=0.9")
                .build()

            client.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
                if (!response.isSuccessful) {
                    return@withContext listOf(resolveLink(cleanUrl, boardName))
                }

                val effectiveUrl = response.request.url.toString()
                val contentType = response.header("Content-Type")?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) ?: ""
                val isDirectImage = contentType.startsWith("image/")

                if (isDirectImage) {
                    return@withContext listOf(resolveLink(cleanUrl, boardName))
                }

                // Read HTML body up to 1 MB for full article / gallery extraction
                val htmlBody = response.body?.source()?.let { source ->
                    source.request(1048576) // 1 MB
                    val buffer = source.buffer
                    val maxBytes = minOf(1048576L, buffer.size)
                    buffer.clone().readUtf8(maxBytes)
                } ?: ""

                val extractedTitle = extractTitleFromHtml(htmlBody).ifBlank { defaultTitle }

                // Extract all candidate images in document order
                val candidateList = extractOrderedContentImages(htmlBody, effectiveUrl)

                if (candidateList.isEmpty()) {
                    return@withContext listOf(resolveLink(cleanUrl, boardName))
                }

                val baseTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                val resultPins = candidateList.mapIndexed { index, cand ->
                    val pinId = generateDeterministicPinId("${cleanUrl}_multi_$index")
                    val itemTitle = if (cand.alt.isNotBlank() && cand.alt.length in 3..120 && !cand.alt.contains("image", ignoreCase = true)) {
                        cand.alt.trim()
                    } else if (index == 0) {
                        extractedTitle
                    } else {
                        "$extractedTitle (${index + 1})"
                    }

                    PinItem(
                        id = pinId,
                        title = itemTitle,
                        description = "Image ${index + 1} of ${candidateList.size} from $domain",
                        imageUrl = cand.url,
                        thumbnailUrl = cand.url,
                        author = domain.ifBlank { "Web Source" },
                        boardName = boardName,
                        sourceUrl = cleanUrl,
                        width = cand.width ?: 1080,
                        height = cand.height ?: 1440,
                        dominantColor = 0xFF2D2D2DL,
                        dateAddedMillis = baseTime + (index * 10L), // Strictly increasing for stable sorting
                        boardPosition = index,
                        fileSizeKb = 250L,
                        mimeType = guessMimeType(cand.url),
                        sourceDomain = domain,
                        isBrokenOrUnreachable = false
                    )
                }

                return@withContext resultPins
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            return@withContext listOf(resolveLink(cleanUrl, boardName))
        }
    }

    fun extractOrderedContentImages(html: String, baseUrl: String): List<ImageCandidate> {
        val seenUrls = mutableSetOf<String>()
        val orderedCandidates = mutableListOf<ImageCandidate>()

        // 1. Check for lead / meta / json-ld / booru images that might appear first
        val leadImages = mutableListOf<ImageCandidate>()
        leadImages.addAll(extractImageBoardImages(html, baseUrl))
        leadImages.addAll(extractMetaImages(html, baseUrl))
        leadImages.addAll(extractJsonLdImages(html, baseUrl))

        val negativeWords = setOf(
            "logo", "icon", "avatar", "profile", "emoji",
            "favicon", "sprite", "badge", "button", "tracking",
            "pixel", "thumb_small", "ad-", "ad_", "advertisement",
            "share-button", "social-share", "navigation", "nav-"
        )

        fun isUnwanted(url: String, alt: String, context: String): Boolean {
            val lower = "$url $alt $context".lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
            if (url.startsWith("data:")) return true
            if (url.endsWith(".svg", ignoreCase = true)) return true
            for (w in negativeWords) {
                if (lower.contains(w)) return true
            }
            return false
        }

        // Add lead images if they are good quality
        for (cand in leadImages) {
            val norm = normalizeUrl(cand.url)
            if (!seenUrls.contains(norm) && !isUnwanted(cand.url, cand.alt, cand.context)) {
                seenUrls.add(norm)
                orderedCandidates.add(cand)
            }
        }

        // 2. Sequential scan of <img> and <a> tags in HTML in document order
        val aTagWithImgRegex = Regex("""<a\b[^>]*href=["']([^"']+\.(?:jpe?g|png|webp|gif|avif)(?:\?[^"']*)?)["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?<img\b[^>]*>)""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
        val linkedMatches = aTagWithImgRegex.findAll(html).toList()
        for (m in linkedMatches) {
            val fullImgUrl = resolveAbsoluteUrl(baseUrl, m.groupValues[1])
            val imgTag = m.groupValues[2]
            val attrs = parseTagAttributes(imgTag)
            val alt = attrs["alt"] ?: ""
            val cls = attrs["class"] ?: ""
            val norm = normalizeUrl(fullImgUrl)
            if (!seenUrls.contains(norm) && !isUnwanted(fullImgUrl, alt, cls)) {
                seenUrls.add(norm)
                orderedCandidates.add(
                    ImageCandidate(
                        url = fullImgUrl,
                        source = "LINKED_IMG",
                        alt = alt,
                        context = cls,
                        sources = mutableSetOf("LINKED_IMG")
                    )
                )
            }
        }

        // 3. Scan all <img> tags in document order
        IMG_TAG_REGEX.findAll(html).forEach { match ->
            val attrs = parseTagAttributes(match.value)
            val rawUrl = getImageUrlFromAttrs(attrs)
            if (!rawUrl.isNullOrBlank() && looksLikeImageUrl(rawUrl)) {
                val fullUrl = resolveAbsoluteUrl(baseUrl, rawUrl)
                val norm = normalizeUrl(fullUrl)
                val alt = attrs["alt"] ?: ""
                val cls = attrs["class"] ?: ""
                val width = attrs["width"]?.toIntOrNull()
                val height = attrs["height"]?.toIntOrNull()

                val tooSmall = (width != null && width < 120) || (height != null && height < 120)

                if (!tooSmall && !seenUrls.contains(norm) && !isUnwanted(fullUrl, alt, cls)) {
                    seenUrls.add(norm)
                    orderedCandidates.add(
                        ImageCandidate(
                            url = fullUrl,
                            source = "IMG",
                            alt = alt,
                            context = cls,
                            width = width,
                            height = height,
                            sources = mutableSetOf("IMG")
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        return orderedCandidates
    }

    /**
     * Browserless two-pass main-image extractor:
     * 1. Extract all candidates from HTML (meta, JSON-LD, imageboard, img, picture/srcset).
     * 2. Calculate cheap preliminary score for each candidate.
     * 3. Sort and take top N candidates (default 5).
     * 4. Download bounded image data for those top candidates and read actual width/height.
     * 5. Recalculate final score with dimensions.
     * 6. Return candidate with the highest final score.
     */
    fun extractMainImageTwoPass(html: String, pageUrl: String): ImageCandidate? {
        val candidates = mutableListOf<ImageCandidate>()

        // 1. Meta images (OpenGraph, Twitter, Thumbnail, Itemprop)
        candidates.addAll(extractMetaImages(html, pageUrl))

        // 2. JSON-LD images
        candidates.addAll(extractJsonLdImages(html, pageUrl))

        // 3. Image board specific patterns
        candidates.addAll(extractImageBoardImages(html, pageUrl))

        // 4. HTML img & picture/source tags
        candidates.addAll(extractHtmlImages(html, pageUrl))

        // Merge duplicates by normalized URL
        val merged = mergeDuplicates(candidates).toMutableList()
        if (merged.isEmpty()) return null

        // Pass 1: Cheap preliminary scoring
        for (candidate in merged) {
