import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.example.model.DateAlbum
import com.example.model.PinterestBoard
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestBlack
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestPillDarkGray
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestPillGray
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestRed
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestTextSecondary
import com.example.util.LinkExtractor

@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)
@Composable
fun AddManualImagesDialog(
    isOpen: Boolean,
    availableBoards: List<PinterestBoard>,
    currentBoardId: String?,
    availableAlbums: List<DateAlbum> = emptyList(),
    currentAlbumId: String? = null,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onAddImages: (rawText: String, targetBoardIds: List<String>, extractMultipleImages: Boolean, putIntoAlbum: Boolean, targetAlbumId: String?) -> Unit
) {
    if (!isOpen) return

    val context = LocalContext.current
    var textInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var extractMultipleImages by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var putIntoAlbum by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var selectedAlbumId by remember(currentAlbumId) { mutableStateOf(currentAlbumId) }

    // Selected boards (defaults to active board or default board)
    val selectedBoardIds = remember(availableBoards, currentBoardId) {
        val initial = mutableStateListOf<String>()
        if (!currentBoardId.isNullOrBlank() && availableBoards.any { it.id == currentBoardId }) {
            initial.add(currentBoardId)
        } else {
            availableBoards.firstOrNull { it.isDefault }?.id?.let { initial.add(it) }
                ?: availableBoards.firstOrNull()?.id?.let { initial.add(it) }
        }
        initial
    }

    val extractedLinks = remember(textInput) {
        LinkExtractor.extractLinks(textInput)
    }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.AddPhotoAlternate,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = PinterestRed,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Add Images Manually",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    fontSize = 18.sp
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Paste image links or webpage URLs (news sites, articles, blogs, image boards). The app extracts images and preserves source links.",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary,
                        fontSize = 13.sp
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = textInput,
                    onValueChange = { textInput = it },
                    label = { Text("Links or Unstructured Text", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    placeholder = { Text("Paste text or links here...", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    minLines = 4,
                    maxLines = 6,
                    supportingText = {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = if (extractedLinks.isNotEmpty()) "Found ${extractedLinks.size} link(s)"
                                else "No links found yet",
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                fontWeight = if (extractedLinks.isNotEmpty()) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                color = if (extractedLinks.isNotEmpty()) PinterestRed else PinterestTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    },
                    trailingIcon = {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as? ClipboardManager
                                val clipText = clipboard?.primaryClip?.getItemAt(0)?.text?.toString()
                                if (!clipText.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                    textInput = if (textInput.isBlank()) clipText.trim()
                                    else textInput + "\n" + clipText.trim()
                                }
                            }
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.ContentPaste,
                                contentDescription = "Paste from Clipboard",
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    },
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = PinterestRed,
                        cursorColor = PinterestRed
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("manual_images_input_field")
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // Checklist 1: Add multiple main image in one webpage (unchecked by default)
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clickable {
                            extractMultipleImages = !extractMultipleImages
                            if (!extractMultipleImages) putIntoAlbum = false
                        }
                        .padding(vertical = 4.dp)
                        .testTag("extract_multiple_images_checkbox")
                ) {
                    Checkbox(
                        checked = extractMultipleImages,
                        onCheckedChange = {
                            extractMultipleImages = it
                            if (!it) putIntoAlbum = false
                        },
                        colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = PinterestRed)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = "Add multiple main images from webpage",
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            color = PinterestBlack
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "Extract all content images from webpage in order (first to last).",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            color = PinterestTextSecondary
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Checklist 2: Shown only when user checks extractMultipleImages
                if (extractMultipleImages) {
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(start = 24.dp)
                            .clickable { putIntoAlbum = !putIntoAlbum }
                            .padding(vertical = 4.dp)
                            .testTag("put_into_album_checkbox")
                    ) {
                        Checkbox(
                            checked = putIntoAlbum,
                            onCheckedChange = { putIntoAlbum = it },
                            colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = PinterestRed)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text(
                                text = "Put into album",
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                color = PinterestBlack
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Group images as an album on the board instead of loading separately.",
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                Text(
                    text = "SELECT BOARDS TO INCLUDE",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                    )
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                FlowRow(
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    availableBoards.forEach { board ->
                        val isChecked = selectedBoardIds.contains(board.id)
                        Surface(
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                            color = if (isChecked) PinterestBlack else PinterestPillGray,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clickable {
                                    if (isChecked) selectedBoardIds.remove(board.id)
                                    else selectedBoardIds.add(board.id)
                                }
                                .testTag("select_target_board_${board.id}")
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                            ) {
                                if (isChecked) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = Color.White,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                }
                                Text(
                                    text = board.title,
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (isChecked) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                    color = if (isChecked) Color.White else PinterestBlack
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // If non-sort custom albums exist, user can add directly to an album
                if (availableAlbums.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "OR ADD DIRECTLY TO ALBUM",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            color = PinterestTextSecondary
                        )
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                    FlowRow(
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    ) {
                        availableAlbums.forEach { album ->
                            val isSelected = selectedAlbumId == album.id
                            Surface(
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                color = if (isSelected) PinterestRed else PinterestPillGray,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clickable {
                                        selectedAlbumId = if (isSelected) null else album.id
                                    }
                                    .testTag("select_target_album_${album.id}")
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Folder,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = if (isSelected) Color.White else PinterestRed,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = album.title,
                                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                        color = if (isSelected) Color.White else PinterestBlack
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Tip: Any other custom boards with matching domains will also receive the images automatically.",
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    color = PinterestTextSecondary
                )
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (extractedLinks.isNotEmpty() && (selectedBoardIds.isNotEmpty() || selectedAlbumId != null)) {
                        onAddImages(
                            textInput.trim(),
                            selectedBoardIds.toList(),
                            extractMultipleImages,
                            putIntoAlbum,
                            selectedAlbumId
                        )
                        onDismiss()
                    }
                },
                enabled = extractedLinks.isNotEmpty() && (selectedBoardIds.isNotEmpty() || selectedAlbumId != null),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("confirm_add_manual_images_btn")
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = if (putIntoAlbum) "Create Album (${extractedLinks.size} URL${if (extractedLinks.size > 1) "s" else ""})"
                    else "Add ${extractedLinks.size} Link${if (extractedLinks.size > 1) "s" else ""}",
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = PinterestBlack)
            }
        },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    )
}

package com.example.ui.dialogs

import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager

import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.height

import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons

import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ContentCopy

enum class BoardTransferMode {
    COPY,
    MOVE
}

@Composable
fun CopyOrMoveImagesDialog(
    isOpen: Boolean,
    mode: BoardTransferMode,
    selectedPins: List<PinItem> = emptyList(),
    selectedCount: Int,
    availableBoards: List<PinterestBoard>,
    currentBoardId: String?,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: (targetBoardIds: List<String>) -> Unit
) {
    if (!isOpen) return

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    val selectableBoards = remember(availableBoards, currentBoardId, mode) {
        if (mode == BoardTransferMode.MOVE && !currentBoardId.isNullOrBlank()) {
            availableBoards.filter { it.id != currentBoardId }
        } else {
            availableBoards
        }
    }

    val selectedBoardIds = remember { mutableStateListOf<String>() }

    val isMove = mode == BoardTransferMode.MOVE
    val actionVerb = if (isMove) "Move" else "Copy"
    val icon = if (isMove) Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.DriveFileMove else Icons.Default.ContentCopy

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        icon = {
            Icon(
                imageVector = icon,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = PinterestRed,
                modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
            )
        },
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "$actionVerb $selectedCount Image${if (selectedCount > 1) "s" else ""}",
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                fontSize = 18.sp
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            ) {
                // Top quick actions for Copy mode (User requirement 2)
                if (mode == BoardTransferMode.COPY && selectedPins.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Text(
                        text = "QUICK ACTIONS",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                            fontSize = 10.sp,
                            color = PinterestTextSecondary
                        )
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                    // 1. Copy links of images
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 3.dp)
                            .clickable {
                                val links = selectedPins.map { it.imageUrl.ifBlank { it.sourceUrl } }.filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                                val text = links.joinToString("\n")
                                val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
                                val clip = ClipData.newPlainText("Image Links", text)
                                clipboard.setPrimaryClip(clip)
                                Toast.makeText(context, "Copied ${links.size} link(s) to clipboard", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                onDismiss()
                            }
                            .testTag("action_copy_links")
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Link,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Copy links of images",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                color = PinterestBlack
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // 2. Copy the image(s) itself to clipboard
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 3.dp)
                            .clickable {
                                coroutineScope.launch {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "Copying image to clipboard...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    val firstPin = selectedPins.firstOrNull()
                                    if (firstPin != null) {
                                        val uri = downloadToCacheUri(context, firstPin)
                                        if (uri != null) {
                                            val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
                                            val clip = ClipData.newUri(context.contentResolver, "Image", uri)
                                            clipboard.setPrimaryClip(clip)
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "Copied image to clipboard", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        } else {
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to copy image", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        }
                                    }
                                    onDismiss()
                                }
                            }
                            .testTag("action_copy_image_itself")
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Image,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Copy the images itself",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                color = PinterestBlack
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // 3. Share the images links
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 3.dp)
                            .clickable {
                                val links = selectedPins.map { it.imageUrl.ifBlank { it.sourceUrl } }.filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                                val text = links.joinToString("\n")
                                val sendIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                                    type = "text/plain"
                                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, text)
                                }
                                val shareIntent = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Image Links")
                                context.startActivity(shareIntent)
                                onDismiss()
                            }
                            .testTag("action_share_links")
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Share,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Share the images links",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                color = PinterestBlack
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // 4. Share the image itself
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 3.dp)
                            .clickable {
                                coroutineScope.launch {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "Preparing images to share...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    val uris = ArrayList<Uri>()
                                    for (pin in selectedPins.take(10)) {
                                        val u = downloadToCacheUri(context, pin)
                                        if (u != null) uris.add(u)
                                    }
                                    if (uris.isNotEmpty()) {
                                        val sendIntent = if (uris.size == 1) {
                                            Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                                                type = "image/jpeg"
                                                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uris[0])
                                                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                                            }
                                        } else {
                                            Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE).apply {
                                                type = "image/jpeg"
                                                putParcelableArrayListExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uris)
                                                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                                            }
                                        }
                                        val shareIntent = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Image")
                                        context.startActivity(shareIntent)
                                    } else {
                                        Toast.makeText(context, "Could not prepare image", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    }
                                    onDismiss()
                                }
                            }
                            .testTag("action_share_image_itself")
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Share,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Share the image itself",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                color = PinterestBlack
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    HorizontalDivider()
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                }

                Text(
                    text = if (isMove) {
                        "Select one or more destination boards. Images will be transferred to selected boards and removed from the current board."
                    } else {
                        "Select one or more destination boards to copy these images into."
                    },
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary,
                        fontSize = 13.sp
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                Text(
                    text = "TARGET BOARDS",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                if (selectableBoards.isEmpty()) {
                    Text(
                        text = "No other boards available. Create a custom board first.",
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                    )
                } else {
                    selectableBoards.forEach { board ->
                        val isChecked = selectedBoardIds.contains(board.id)
                        Surface(
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                            color = if (isChecked) PinterestRed.copy(alpha = 0.1f) else PinterestPillGray,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(vertical = 3.dp)
                                .clickable {
                                    if (isChecked) selectedBoardIds.remove(board.id)
                                    else selectedBoardIds.add(board.id)
                                }
                                .testTag("transfer_board_item_${board.id}")
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = board.title,
                                        fontWeight = if (isChecked) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                                        color = if (isChecked) PinterestRed else PinterestBlack
                                    )
                                    if (board.isCustom) {
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                        Surface(
                                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(6.dp),
                                            color = PinterestRed.copy(alpha = 0.15f)
                                        ) {
                                            Text(
                                                text = "Custom",
                                                color = PinterestRed,
                                                fontSize = 9.sp,
                                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }

                                if (isChecked) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                                        contentDescription = "Selected",
                                        tint = PinterestRed,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (selectedBoardIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                        onConfirm(selectedBoardIds.toList())
                        onDismiss()
                    }
                },
                enabled = selectedBoardIds.isNotEmpty(),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("confirm_transfer_btn")
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "$actionVerb to ${selectedBoardIds.size} Board${if (selectedBoardIds.size > 1) "s" else ""}",
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = PinterestBlack)
            }
        },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    )
}

private suspend fun downloadToCacheUri(context: Context, pin: PinItem): Uri? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
    try {
        val client = OkHttpClient()
        val request = Request.Builder().url(pin.imageUrl).build()
        val response = client.newCall(request).execute()
        if (!response.isSuccessful || response.body == null) return@withContext null

        val cacheDir = File(context.cacheDir, "shared_images").apply { if (!exists()) mkdirs() }
        val file = File(cacheDir, "share_${pin.id.take(20)}.jpg")
        FileOutputStream(file).use { out ->
            response.body!!.byteStream().copyTo(out)
        }

        FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", file)
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        null
    }
}

@Composable
fun CreateCustomBoardDialog(
    isOpen: Boolean,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onCreateBoard: (name: String, domains: List<String>, unstructuredLinksText: String, isDefault: Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    if (!isOpen) return

    val context = LocalContext.current
    var boardName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var customDomains by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var unstructuredLinks by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var isDefault by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val detectedLinksCount = remember(unstructuredLinks) {
        LinkExtractor.extractLinks(unstructuredLinks).size
    }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.ViewCarousel,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = PinterestRed,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "New Custom Board",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    fontSize = 18.sp
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Create a custom board to organize your own direct image links.",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary,
                        fontSize = 13.sp
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // 1. Board Name (Required)
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = boardName,
                    onValueChange = { boardName = it },
                    label = { Text("Board Name *", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    placeholder = { Text("e.g. Wallpapers, Design Ideas", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    singleLine = true,
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = PinterestRed,
                        cursorColor = PinterestRed
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("create_custom_board_name_field")
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // 2. Custom Web Domains (Optional)
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = customDomains,
                    onValueChange = { customDomains = it },
                    label = { Text("Custom Web Domains (Optional)", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    placeholder = { Text("e.g. opu.com, unsplash.com", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    supportingText = {
                        Text(
                            text = "When new links are added anywhere matching these domains, images will automatically be saved to this board without duplicates.",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            color = PinterestTextSecondary
                        )
                    },
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = PinterestRed,
                        cursorColor = PinterestRed
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    trailingIcon = {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Domain,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = PinterestTextSecondary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("create_custom_board_domains_field")
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // 3. Initial Image Links / Unstructured text (Optional)
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = unstructuredLinks,
                    onValueChange = { unstructuredLinks = it },
                    label = { Text("Add Image Links (Optional)", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    placeholder = { Text("Paste text with one or more image links...", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    minLines = 3,
                    maxLines = 5,
                    supportingText = {
                        if (detectedLinksCount > 0) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Found $detectedLinksCount link${if (detectedLinksCount > 1) "s" else ""}",
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = PinterestRed,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        } else {
                            Text(
                                text = "Accepts free-form text containing multiple URLs",
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    },
                    trailingIcon = {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as? ClipboardManager
                                val clipText = clipboard?.primaryClip?.getItemAt(0)?.text?.toString()
                                if (!clipText.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                    unstructuredLinks = if (unstructuredLinks.isBlank()) clipText.trim()
                                    else unstructuredLinks + "\n" + clipText.trim()
                                }
                            }
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.ContentPaste,
                                contentDescription = "Paste from Clipboard",
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    },
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = PinterestRed,
                        cursorColor = PinterestRed
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("create_custom_board_links_field")
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                // 4. Default Board Switch
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Set as Default Board",
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            fontSize = 13.sp
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "Images shared from other apps will default to this board",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            color = PinterestTextSecondary
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Switch(
                        checked = isDefault,
                        onCheckedChange = { isDefault = it },
                        colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                            checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                            checkedTrackColor = PinterestRed
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (boardName.isNotBlank()) {
                        val domainList = customDomains.split(",", "\n", " ")
                            .map { it.trim() }
                            .filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                        onCreateBoard(boardName.trim(), domainList, unstructuredLinks.trim(), isDefault)
                        onDismiss()
                    }
                },
                enabled = boardName.isNotBlank(),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("create_custom_board_confirm_btn")
            ) {
                Text("Create Board", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = PinterestBlack)
            }
        },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun EditCustomBoardDialog(
    isOpen: Boolean,
    board: PinterestBoard?,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSaveBoard: (boardId: String, newName: String, domains: List<String>, isDefault: Boolean) -> Unit,
    onDeleteBoard: (PinterestBoard) -> Unit,
    onOpenReorderBoards: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    if (!isOpen || board == null) return

    var boardName by remember(board) { mutableStateOf(board.title) }
    var customDomains by remember(board) { mutableStateOf(board.customDomains.joinToString(", ")) }
    var isDefault by remember(board) { mutableStateOf(board.isDefault) }
    var showDeleteConfirm by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    if (showDeleteConfirm) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showDeleteConfirm = false },
            icon = {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Warning,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = PinterestRed,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                )
            },
            title = {
                Text(
                    text = "Delete \"${board.title}\"?",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    fontSize = 17.sp
                )
            },
            text = {
                Text(
                    text = "Are you sure you want to delete this custom board and all its images? You can undo this immediately from the toast message.",
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    color = PinterestTextSecondary
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        showDeleteConfirm = false
                        onDismiss()
                        onDeleteBoard(board)
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("confirm_delete_custom_board_btn")
                ) {
                    Text("Delete Board", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { showDeleteConfirm = false }) {
                    Text("Cancel", color = PinterestBlack)
                }
            },
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
        )
        return
    }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Edit,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = PinterestRed,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Edit Board",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    fontSize = 18.sp
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            ) {
                // Name
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = boardName,
                    onValueChange = { boardName = it },
                    label = { Text("Board Name", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    singleLine = true,
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = PinterestRed,
                        cursorColor = PinterestRed
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("edit_custom_board_name_field")
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // Custom Domains
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = customDomains,
                    onValueChange = { customDomains = it },
                    label = { Text("Custom Web Domains", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    placeholder = { Text("e.g. opu.com, unsplash.com", fontSize = 13.sp) },
                    supportingText = {
                        Text(
                            text = "Matching images added anywhere will automatically join this board without duplicates.",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            color = PinterestTextSecondary
                        )
                    },
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = PinterestRed,
                        cursorColor = PinterestRed
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    trailingIcon = {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Domain,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = PinterestTextSecondary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("edit_custom_board_domains_field")
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // Default Board Switch (not applicable to Favorites)
                if (board.id != "board_favorites") {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Default Board for Saves",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = if (isDefault) "Currently set as default" else "Images without board choice save here",
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Switch(
                            checked = isDefault,
                            onCheckedChange = { isDefault = it },
                            colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                                checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                                checkedTrackColor = PinterestRed
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Reorder Boards Button (Requirement 6)
                Surface(
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    color = PinterestPillGray,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clickable { onOpenReorderBoards() }
                        .testTag("open_reorder_boards_btn")
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.SwapVert,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestRed,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Column {
                                Text(
                                    text = "Reorder Boards",
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                                    color = PinterestBlack
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "Custom drag/sort order & board type rules",
                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                    color = PinterestTextSecondary
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.SwapVert,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = PinterestTextSecondary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))
                HorizontalDivider()
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // Deletion Section
                if (board.isSpecialNonDeletable) {
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.Lock,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = PinterestTextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "System board (cannot be deleted)",
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                            )
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    OutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { showDeleteConfirm = true },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(
                            contentColor = PinterestRed
                        ),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .testTag("delete_custom_board_btn")
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Delete,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text("Delete Board", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (boardName.isNotBlank()) {
                        val domainList = customDomains.split(",", "\n", " ")
                            .map { it.trim() }
                            .filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                        onSaveBoard(board.id, boardName.trim(), domainList, isDefault)
                        onDismiss()
                    }
                },
                enabled = boardName.isNotBlank(),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("save_custom_board_btn")
            ) {
                Text("Save Changes", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = PinterestBlack)
            }
        },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    )
}

package com.example.ui.dialogs

import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement

import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.example.model.BoardHistoryItem
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestBlack
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestPillGray
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestRed
import com.example.ui.theme.PinterestTextSecondary

enum class BoardSortPreset {
    NONE,
    ALPHABET_ASC,
    ALPHABET_DESC,
    DATE_NEWEST,
    DATE_OLDEST,
    ACTIVITY_HIGH,
    ACTIVITY_LOW
}

@Composable
fun ReorderBoardsDialog(
    isOpen: Boolean,
    boards: List<BoardHistoryItem>,
    forceBoardTypeOrder: Boolean,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSaveReorderedBoards: (reorderedList: List<BoardHistoryItem>, forceTypeOrder: Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    if (!isOpen) return

    var forceTypeOrderState by remember(forceBoardTypeOrder) { mutableStateOf(forceBoardTypeOrder) }
    val currentList = remember(boards) { mutableStateListOf<BoardHistoryItem>().apply { addAll(boards) } }

    fun applyBoardTypeConstraints(input: List<BoardHistoryItem>): List<BoardHistoryItem> {
        if (!forceTypeOrderState) return input

        // Tier 1: Pinterest Boards
        val pinterest = input.filter {
            !it.isCustom && it.boardId != "board_dump" && it.boardId != "board_my_save" && it.boardId != "board_favorites" &&
                    !it.url.startsWith("custom://")
        }

        // Tier 2: Permanent custom boards strictly ordered: 1. Dump, 2. My Save, 3. Favorites
        val dump = input.firstOrNull { it.boardId == "board_dump" || it.url == "custom://dump" }
        val mySave = input.firstOrNull { it.boardId == "board_my_save" || it.url == "custom://my_save" }
        val fav = input.firstOrNull { it.boardId == "board_favorites" || it.url == "custom://favorites" }
        val permanent = listOfNotNull(dump, mySave, fav)

        // Tier 3: Other Custom Boards
        val custom = input.filter {
            it.isCustom && it.boardId != "board_dump" && it.boardId != "board_my_save" && it.boardId != "board_favorites" &&
                    it.url != "custom://dump" && it.url != "custom://my_save" && it.url != "custom://favorites"
        }

        return pinterest + permanent + custom
    }

    fun sortList(preset: BoardSortPreset) {
        val sorted = when (preset) {
            BoardSortPreset.NONE -> currentList.toList()
            BoardSortPreset.ALPHABET_ASC -> currentList.sortedBy { it.title.lowercase() }
            BoardSortPreset.ALPHABET_DESC -> currentList.sortedByDescending { it.title.lowercase() }
            BoardSortPreset.DATE_NEWEST -> currentList.sortedByDescending { it.timestamp }
            BoardSortPreset.DATE_OLDEST -> currentList.sortedBy { it.timestamp }
            BoardSortPreset.ACTIVITY_HIGH -> currentList.sortedByDescending { it.pinCount }
            BoardSortPreset.ACTIVITY_LOW -> currentList.sortedBy { it.pinCount }
        }
        val constrained = if (forceTypeOrderState) applyBoardTypeConstraints(sorted) else sorted
        currentList.clear()
        currentList.addAll(constrained)
    }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.SwapVert,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = PinterestRed,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Reorder Boards",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    fontSize = 18.sp
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Rearrange the boards list order or sort them automatically.",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = PinterestTextSecondary
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // Force Board Type Order Checklist (Requirement 6)
                Surface(
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    color = PinterestPillGray,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clickable {
                                forceTypeOrderState = !forceTypeOrderState
                                if (forceTypeOrderState) {
                                    val constrained = applyBoardTypeConstraints(currentList.toList())
                                    currentList.clear()
                                    currentList.addAll(constrained)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Checkbox(
                            checked = forceTypeOrderState,
                            onCheckedChange = { checked ->
                                forceTypeOrderState = checked
                                if (checked) {
                                    val constrained = applyBoardTypeConstraints(currentList.toList())
                                    currentList.clear()
                                    currentList.addAll(constrained)
                                }
                            },
                            colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = PinterestRed),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("force_board_type_order_checkbox")
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Force boards type order",
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                color = PinterestBlack
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Hold order: 1. Pinterest  2. Permanent (Dump, My Save, Favorites)  3. Custom boards",
                                fontSize = 10.sp,
                                color = PinterestTextSecondary,
                                lineHeight = 13.sp
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // Sort Preset Quick Buttons
                Text(
                    text = "SORT BY PRESET",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        letterSpacing = 0.6.sp,
                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                ) {
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .clickable { sortList(BoardSortPreset.ALPHABET_ASC) }
                            .testTag("sort_alphabet_asc_btn")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "A-Z",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 6.dp)
                        )
                    }

                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .clickable { sortList(BoardSortPreset.ALPHABET_DESC) }
                            .testTag("sort_alphabet_desc_btn")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Z-A",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 6.dp)
                        )
                    }

                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1.3f)
                            .clickable { sortList(BoardSortPreset.DATE_NEWEST) }
                            .testTag("sort_date_newest_btn")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Date (New)",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 6.dp)
                        )
                    }

                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = PinterestPillGray,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1.3f)
                            .clickable { sortList(BoardSortPreset.ACTIVITY_HIGH) }
                            .testTag("sort_activity_high_btn")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Active",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 6.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                HorizontalDivider()
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                // Board items list with manual move Up / Down
                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .heightIn(max = 240.dp)
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(
                        items = currentList,
                        key = { idx, item -> "${item.url}_$idx" }
                    ) { index, item ->
                        val isPermanent = item.boardId == "board_dump" || item.boardId == "board_my_save" || item.boardId == "board_favorites"
                        Surface(
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                            color = PinterestPillGray,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(vertical = 2.dp)
                                .testTag("reorder_board_item_$index")
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "${index + 1}.",
                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = PinterestTextSecondary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.width(22.dp)
                                )

                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = when {
                                        item.boardId == "board_favorites" -> Icons.Default.Favorite
                                        item.boardId == "board_my_save" -> Icons.Default.Bookmark
                                        item.boardId == "board_dump" -> Icons.Default.Folder
                                        item.isCustom -> Icons.Default.Folder
                                        else -> Icons.Default.Link
                                    },
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = PinterestRed,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                )

                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))

                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = item.title,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                                        color = PinterestBlack,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = if (isPermanent) "Permanent" else if (item.isCustom) "Custom" else "Pinterest",
                                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                                        color = PinterestTextSecondary
                                    )
                                }

                                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                    IconButton(
                                        onClick = {
                                            if (index > 0) {
                                                val moved = currentList.removeAt(index)
                                                currentList.add(index - 1, moved)
                                                if (forceTypeOrderState) {
                                                    val constrained = applyBoardTypeConstraints(currentList.toList())
                                                    currentList.clear()
                                                    currentList.addAll(constrained)
                                                }
                                            }
                                        },
                                        enabled = index > 0,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Default.KeyboardArrowUp,
                                            contentDescription = "Move Up",
                                            tint = if (index > 0) PinterestBlack else Color.LightGray,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                        )
                                    }

                                    IconButton(
                                        onClick = {
                                            if (index < currentList.size - 1) {
                                                val moved = currentList.removeAt(index)
                                                currentList.add(index + 1, moved)
                                                if (forceTypeOrderState) {
                                                    val constrained = applyBoardTypeConstraints(currentList.toList())
                                                    currentList.clear()
                                                    currentList.addAll(constrained)
                                                }
                                            }
                                        },
                                        enabled = index < currentList.size - 1,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Default.KeyboardArrowDown,
                                            contentDescription = "Move Down",
                                            tint = if (index < currentList.size - 1) PinterestBlack else Color.LightGray,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    val finalResult = if (forceTypeOrderState) applyBoardTypeConstraints(currentList.toList()) else currentList.toList()
                    onSaveReorderedBoards(finalResult, forceTypeOrderState)
                    onDismiss()
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = PinterestRed),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("save_reorder_boards_btn")
            ) {
                Text("Save Order", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = PinterestBlack)
            }
        },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    )
}

--- FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\ui\theme\Theme.kt ---
package com.example.ui.theme

import android.os.Build
import androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.dynamicDarkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.dynamicLightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.lightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext

private val DarkColorScheme =
  darkColorScheme(
    primary = PinterestRed,
    onPrimary = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White,
    primaryContainer = PinterestDarkRed,
    onPrimaryContainer = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White,
    secondary = PinterestLightRed,
    onSecondary = PinterestBlack,
    surface = PinterestDarkSurface,
    onSurface = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White,
    surfaceVariant = PinterestDarkCard,
    onSurfaceVariant = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFCCCCCC),
    background = PinterestBlack,
    onBackground = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White,
  )

private val LightColorScheme =
  lightColorScheme(
    primary = PinterestRed,
    onPrimary = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White,
    primaryContainer = PinterestLightRed,
    onPrimaryContainer = PinterestDarkRed,
    secondary = PinterestRed,
    onSecondary = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.White,
    surface = PinterestLightSurface,
    onSurface = PinterestTextPrimary,
    surfaceVariant = PinterestLightCard,
    onSurfaceVariant = PinterestTextSecondary,
    background = PinterestLightBg,
    onBackground = PinterestTextPrimary,
  )

@Composable
fun MyApplicationTheme(
  darkTheme: Boolean = isSystemInDarkTheme(),
  // Dynamic color is available on Android 12+
  dynamicColor: Boolean = true,
  content: @Composable () -> Unit,
) {
  val colorScheme =
    when {
      dynamicColor && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S -> {
        val context = LocalContext.current
        if (darkTheme) dynamicDarkColorScheme(context) else dynamicLightColorScheme(context)
      }

      darkTheme -> DarkColorScheme
      else -> LightColorScheme
    }

  MaterialTheme(colorScheme = colorScheme, typography = Typography, content = content)
}

  )
